## Decision Memo

Bucket counts over the full filesystem-plus-registry enumeration:

- SAFE: 188
- BACKFILLABLE: 118
- AMBIGUOUS: 1,975

Readable-store subset:

- SAFE: 188
- BACKFILLABLE: 118
- AMBIGUOUS: 0

All 118 fallback-would-fire cases are BACKFILLABLE: the legacy pointer names an existing complete run, `latest` agrees with the legacy value, and removing the legacy key does not change either measured reader winner. No store had a batch-pipeline or task-generation winner change.

All 1,975 AMBIGUOUS rows are unreadable registry paths, not readable pointer conflicts. The unreadable rows are stale or absent registry paths from `/tmp/pytest-of-delahantyj` (1,938), `/tmp` (27), and `/home` worktree/in-memory stores (10). Each verbatim unreadable path/error is listed above.

Recommendation: **keep** for the full enumerated registry until the unreadable registry rows are either cleaned, scoped out by policy, or accepted as non-real stale registry detritus. For the readable mounted store set, the data supports **backfill-then-retire** rather than retire-now: 118 readable stores still rely on `detect_review_status_latest` for `run_resolution` authoritative fallback, but every one is mechanical and unambiguous.

Writer-side premise re-grep:

```text
$ rg -n "attrs\[\"detect_review_status_latest\"\]\s*=|detect_review_status_latest.*help=|--no-pointer-update" src/fisheye/utils/migrate_legacy_detect_labels.py src/fisheye/utils/migrate_refined_detect_sparse.py src/fisheye/utils/migrate_refined_detect_sparse_batch.py
src/fisheye/utils/migrate_refined_detect_sparse.py:666:        refined_parent.attrs["detect_review_status_latest"] = plan.output_refined_run_name
src/fisheye/utils/migrate_legacy_detect_labels.py:526:        parent.attrs["detect_review_status_latest"] = plan.output_refined_run
```

The assignment premise still holds: the only detected writer assignments are the two sanctioned migrations. A broader literal-string grep also finds the known reader sites, schema documentation, the new diagnostic, the batch-wrapper help text, and one additional non-writer help-text mention in `migrate_refined_detect_sparse.py` (`--no-promote-latest` says it will not update `latest` or `detect_review_status_latest`). That help-text mention is a premise discrepancy relative to the brief's "batch wrapper help text" wording, but it is not a writer.

Validation:

- Rebase check: local `sun` was unchanged before final validation (`7ddaf61`), so no rebase was needed.
- `scripts/py -m py_compile src/fisheye/diagnostics/detect_review_pointer_census.py tests/unit/fisheye/test_detect_review_pointer_census.py`: passed.
- `PYTHONPATH=src scripts/py -m pytest -q tests/unit/fisheye/test_detect_review_pointer_census.py`: 3 passed.
- `git diff --check`: passed.
- `scripts/py -m importlinter.cli --config pyproject.toml`: passed.
- `scripts/py scripts/check_file_size_ratchet.py`: passed; file-size ratchet reported 4 files and made no tracked changes.
- `PYTHONPATH=src scripts/py -m pytest tests -m "not gpu" -q -n 16`: 3,446 passed / 2 skipped / 233 warnings in 65.37s.

Baseline in the brief was 3,451 passed / 2 skipped at `c57e810`; current local `sun`/branch recount is 3,446 passed / 2 skipped.
